8:T6100,<pre class="shiki github-dark-default" style="background-color:#0d1117;color:#e6edf3" tabindex="0"><code><span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E">/**</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E"> * Detección de soporte y disponibilidad para cada API built-in.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E"> *</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E"> * Regla de oro de la charla: NUNCA asumas que el modelo está listo.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E"> *   1. Feature detection  → ¿existe la API en este navegador?   ('X' in self)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E"> *   2. availability()     → ¿este equipo puede usarla ya?</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E"> *                           'available' | 'downloadable' | 'downloading' | 'unavailable'</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E"> *   3. create()           → dentro de un gesto del usuario (clic, tecla) si hay que descargar.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E"> *</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E"> * Los globals de IA built-in (LanguageModel, Translator…) solo existen en el</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E"> * navegador. Llama a estos helpers desde efectos o handlers, nunca durante el</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E"> * render ni en SSR.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E"> */</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">export</span><span style="color:#FF7B72"> type</span><span style="color:#FFA657"> ApiName</span><span style="color:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="color:#A5D6FF"> 'LanguageModel'</span><span style="color:#FF7B72"> |</span><span style="color:#A5D6FF"> 'Summarizer'</span><span style="color:#FF7B72"> |</span><span style="color:#A5D6FF"> 'Rewriter'</span><span style="color:#FF7B72"> |</span><span style="color:#A5D6FF"> 'Translator'</span><span style="color:#FF7B72"> |</span><span style="color:#A5D6FF"> 'LanguageDetector'</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">;</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E">/** Los cuatro estados oficiales de availability() + uno propio: el navegador ni siquiera tiene la API. */</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">export</span><span style="color:#FF7B72"> type</span><span style="color:#FFA657"> ApiStatus</span><span style="color:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="color:#FFA657"> Availability</span><span style="color:#FF7B72"> |</span><span style="color:#A5D6FF"> 'no-soportada'</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">;</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E">/** Idioma usado en toda la charla (español). availability() depende del idioma que declares. */</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">export</span><span style="color:#FF7B72"> const</span><span style="color:#79C0FF"> ES</span><span style="color:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> [</span><span style="color:#A5D6FF">'es'</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">];</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E">/** Paso 1 · Feature detection segura para SSR (`typeof self` evita el ReferenceError en el servidor). */</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">export</span><span style="color:#FF7B72"> function</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF"> hasApi</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">(</span><span style="color:#FFA657">name</span><span style="color:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="color:#FFA657"> ApiName</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">)</span><span style="color:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="color:#79C0FF"> boolean</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">  return</span><span style="color:#FF7B72"> typeof</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> self </span><span style="color:#FF7B72">!==</span><span style="color:#A5D6FF"> 'undefined'</span><span style="color:#FF7B72"> &#x26;&#x26;</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> name </span><span style="color:#FF7B72">in</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> self;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">}</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E">/**</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E"> * Paso 2 · availability() con LAS MISMAS opciones que usará create().</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E"> * La respuesta cambia según idioma y modalidad: preguntar sin opciones puede</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E"> * decir 'available' y luego create() fallar con el idioma real.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E"> */</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">export</span><span style="color:#FF7B72"> async</span><span style="color:#FF7B72"> function</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF"> checkApi</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">(</span><span style="color:#FFA657">name</span><span style="color:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="color:#FFA657"> ApiName</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">)</span><span style="color:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="color:#FFA657"> Promise</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">&#x3C;</span><span style="color:#FFA657">ApiStatus</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">  if</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> (</span><span style="color:#FF7B72">!</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF">hasApi</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">(name)) </span><span style="color:#FF7B72">return</span><span style="color:#A5D6FF"> 'no-soportada'</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">  try</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">    switch</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> (name) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">      case</span><span style="color:#A5D6FF"> 'LanguageModel'</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">:</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">        return</span><span style="color:#FF7B72"> await</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> LanguageModel.</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF">availability</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">({</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">          expectedInputs: [{ type: </span><span style="color:#A5D6FF">'text'</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">, languages: </span><span style="color:#79C0FF">ES</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> }],</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">          expectedOutputs: [{ type: </span><span style="color:#A5D6FF">'text'</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">, languages: </span><span style="color:#79C0FF">ES</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> }],</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">        });</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">      case</span><span style="color:#A5D6FF"> 'Summarizer'</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">:</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">        return</span><span style="color:#FF7B72"> await</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> Summarizer.</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF">availability</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">({ expectedInputLanguages: [</span><span style="color:#A5D6FF">'es'</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">, </span><span style="color:#A5D6FF">'en'</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">], outputLanguage: </span><span style="color:#A5D6FF">'es'</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> });</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">      case</span><span style="color:#A5D6FF"> 'Rewriter'</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">:</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">        return</span><span style="color:#FF7B72"> await</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> Rewriter.</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF">availability</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">({ expectedInputLanguages: </span><span style="color:#79C0FF">ES</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">, outputLanguage: </span><span style="color:#A5D6FF">'es'</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> });</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">      case</span><span style="color:#A5D6FF"> 'Translator'</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">:</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E">        // Solo consulta el par en→es (el del gancho). Cada par de idiomas tiene su propio paquete.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">        return</span><span style="color:#FF7B72"> await</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> Translator.</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF">availability</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">({ sourceLanguage: </span><span style="color:#A5D6FF">'en'</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">, targetLanguage: </span><span style="color:#A5D6FF">'es'</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> });</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">      case</span><span style="color:#A5D6FF"> 'LanguageDetector'</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">:</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">        return</span><span style="color:#FF7B72"> await</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> LanguageDetector.</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF">availability</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">();</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">    }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">  } </span><span style="color:#FF7B72">catch</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E">    // Opciones no soportadas o bloqueo por política: se muestra como 'unavailable', la tabla no se rompe.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">    return</span><span style="color:#A5D6FF"> 'unavailable'</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">  }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">}</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E">/** Orden de las filas en la tabla de la Demo 01. */</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">export</span><span style="color:#FF7B72"> const</span><span style="color:#79C0FF"> API_NAMES</span><span style="color:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="color:#FFA657"> ApiName</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">[] </span><span style="color:#FF7B72">=</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> [</span><span style="color:#A5D6FF">'LanguageModel'</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">, </span><span style="color:#A5D6FF">'LanguageDetector'</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">, </span><span style="color:#A5D6FF">'Translator'</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">, </span><span style="color:#A5D6FF">'Summarizer'</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">, </span><span style="color:#A5D6FF">'Rewriter'</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">];</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E">/**</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E"> * Monitor reutilizable que reporta el progreso de descarga (0 → 1).</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E"> * Se pasa como `monitor` a cualquier create(). Es lo que alimenta la barra de</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E"> * la Demo 01: "el usuario ve qué está pasando, no una pantalla congelada".</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E"> */</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">export</span><span style="color:#FF7B72"> function</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF"> progressMonitor</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">(</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF">onProgress</span><span style="color:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> (</span><span style="color:#FFA657">fraction</span><span style="color:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="color:#79C0FF"> number</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">) </span><span style="color:#FF7B72">=></span><span style="color:#79C0FF"> void</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">)</span><span style="color:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="color:#FFA657"> CreateMonitorCallback</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">  return</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> (</span><span style="color:#FFA657">monitor</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">) </span><span style="color:#FF7B72">=></span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">    monitor.</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF">addEventListener</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">(</span><span style="color:#A5D6FF">'downloadprogress'</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">, (</span><span style="color:#FFA657">event</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">) </span><span style="color:#FF7B72">=></span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E">      // En la spec actual `loaded` ya es una fracción de 0 a 1 (no bytes): no hay que dividir por `total`.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E">      // Si el modelo ya está descargado, este evento puede no llegar nunca.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D2A8FF">      onProgress</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">((event </span><span style="color:#FF7B72">as</span><span style="color:#FFA657"> ProgressEvent</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">).loaded);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">    });</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">  };</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">}</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E">/**</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E"> * Cachea una instancia por clave. Crear la instancia es lo caro (puede</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E"> * descargar un modelo); usarla es barato. Por eso se reutiliza.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E"> *</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E"> * - Se cachea la PROMESA: un doble clic espera la misma creación, no lanza dos.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E"> * - Una creación rechazada se expulsa para que el siguiente clic reintente</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E"> *   (p. ej. el primer intento no tenía activación del usuario → NotAllowedError).</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E"> */</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">export</span><span style="color:#FF7B72"> function</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF"> memoize</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">&#x3C;</span><span style="color:#FFA657">K</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">, </span><span style="color:#FFA657">V</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">>(</span><span style="color:#FFA657">cache</span><span style="color:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="color:#FFA657"> Map</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">&#x3C;</span><span style="color:#FFA657">K</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">, </span><span style="color:#FFA657">Promise</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">&#x3C;</span><span style="color:#FFA657">V</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">>>, </span><span style="color:#FFA657">key</span><span style="color:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="color:#FFA657"> K</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">, </span><span style="color:#D2A8FF">create</span><span style="color:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> () </span><span style="color:#FF7B72">=></span><span style="color:#FFA657"> Promise</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">&#x3C;</span><span style="color:#FFA657">V</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">>)</span><span style="color:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="color:#FFA657"> Promise</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">&#x3C;</span><span style="color:#FFA657">V</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">  let</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> entry </span><span style="color:#FF7B72">=</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> cache.</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF">get</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">(key);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">  if</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> (</span><span style="color:#FF7B72">!</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">entry) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">    entry </span><span style="color:#FF7B72">=</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF"> create</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">();</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">    cache.</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF">set</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">(key, entry);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">    const</span><span style="color:#79C0FF"> created</span><span style="color:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> entry;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E">    // Solo expulsa SU entrada: un reset puede haberla reemplazado ya por una nueva.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">    created.</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF">catch</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">(() </span><span style="color:#FF7B72">=></span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">      if</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> (cache.</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF">get</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">(key) </span><span style="color:#FF7B72">===</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> created) cache.</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF">delete</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">(key);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">    });</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">  }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">  return</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> entry;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">}</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E">/**</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E"> * Destruye y expulsa todas las instancias cacheadas. Se usa tras un fallo en</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E"> * tiempo de ejecución (p. ej. UnknownError) para que el siguiente intento se</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E"> * cure solo, sin recargar la página. Clave para sobrevivir a una demo en vivo.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E"> */</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">export</span><span style="color:#FF7B72"> function</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF"> clearCache</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">&#x3C;</span><span style="color:#FFA657">K</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">, </span><span style="color:#FFA657">V</span><span style="color:#FF7B72"> extends</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> { </span><span style="color:#D2A8FF">destroy</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">()</span><span style="color:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="color:#79C0FF"> void</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> }>(</span><span style="color:#FFA657">cache</span><span style="color:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="color:#FFA657"> Map</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">&#x3C;</span><span style="color:#FFA657">K</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">, </span><span style="color:#FFA657">Promise</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">&#x3C;</span><span style="color:#FFA657">V</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">>>)</span><span style="color:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="color:#79C0FF"> void</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">  for</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> (</span><span style="color:#FF7B72">const</span><span style="color:#79C0FF"> entry</span><span style="color:#FF7B72"> of</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> cache.</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF">values</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">()) {</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E"> * Pinta un stream de texto sin bloquear la UI.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E"> *</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E"> * Las APIs built-in no corren en Web Workers (Summarizer, por ejemplo), así que</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">  let</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> finished </span><span style="color:#FF7B72">=</span><span style="color:#79C0FF"> false</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">;</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">  };</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">        console.</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF">info</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">(</span><span style="color:#A5D6FF">`LanguageDetector: ${</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">Math</span><span style="color:#A5D6FF">.</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF">round</span><span style="color:#A5D6FF">(</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">f</span><span style="color:#FF7B72"> *</span><span style="color:#79C0FF"> 100</span><span style="color:#A5D6FF">)</span><span style="color:#A5D6FF">}%`</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">);</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">      }),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">    });</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">    detector </span><span style="color:#FF7B72">=</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> created;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E">    // Solo limpia su propia promesa: un reset puede haberla reemplazado ya.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">    created.</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF">catch</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">(() </span><span style="color:#FF7B72">=></span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">      if</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> (detector </span><span style="color:#FF7B72">===</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> created) detector </span><span style="color:#FF7B72">=</span><span style="color:#79C0FF"> undefined</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">    });</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">  }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E">  // detect() devuelve candidatos ordenados por confianza; nos quedamos con el primero.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">  const</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> [</span><span style="color:#79C0FF">best</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">] </span><span style="color:#FF7B72">=</span><span style="color:#FF7B72"> await</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> (</span><span style="color:#FF7B72">await</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> detector).</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF">detect</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">(text, { signal });</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">  return</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E">    // Sin resultado (texto muy corto) asumimos inglés.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">    language: best?.detectedLanguage </span><span style="color:#FF7B72">??</span><span style="color:#A5D6FF"> 'en'</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">    confidence: Math.</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF">round</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">((best?.confidence </span><span style="color:#FF7B72">??</span><span style="color:#79C0FF"> 0</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">) </span><span style="color:#FF7B72">*</span><span style="color:#79C0FF"> 100</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">  };</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">}</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E">/**</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E"> * Descarta una instancia cacheada tras un fallo en tiempo de ejecución (p. ej.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E"> * UnknownError) para que el siguiente intento cree una nueva en vez de</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E"> * reutilizar una rota. Los fallos de creación ya los expulsa memoize / el catch del detector.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E"> */</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">export</span><span style="color:#FF7B72"> function</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF"> resetDetector</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">() {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">  void</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> detector?.</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF">then</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">((</span><span style="color:#FFA657">d</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">) </span><span style="color:#FF7B72">=></span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> d.</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF">destroy</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">()).</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF">catch</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">(() </span><span style="color:#FF7B72">=></span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> {});</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">  detector </span><span style="color:#FF7B72">=</span><span style="color:#79C0FF"> undefined</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">}</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">export</span><span style="color:#FF7B72"> function</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF"> resetTranslator</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">(</span><span style="color:#FFA657">sourceLanguage</span><span style="color:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="color:#79C0FF"> string</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">, </span><span style="color:#FFA657">targetLanguage</span><span style="color:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="color:#79C0FF"> string</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">  const</span><span style="color:#79C0FF"> key</span><span style="color:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="color:#A5D6FF"> `${</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">sourceLanguage</span><span style="color:#A5D6FF">}→${</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">targetLanguage</span><span style="color:#A5D6FF">}`</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">  void</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> translators.</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF">get</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">(key)?.</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF">then</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">((</span><span style="color:#FFA657">t</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">) </span><span style="color:#FF7B72">=></span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> t.</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF">destroy</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">()).</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF">catch</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">(() </span><span style="color:#FF7B72">=></span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> {});</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">  translators.</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF">delete</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">(key);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">}</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E">/** availability() por par → Translator cacheado → stream de texto traducido. */</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">export</span><span style="color:#FF7B72"> async</span><span style="color:#FF7B72"> function</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF"> translateStreaming</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">(</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FFA657">  text</span><span style="color:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="color:#79C0FF"> string</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FFA657">  source</span><span style="color:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="color:#79C0FF"> string</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FFA657">  target</span><span style="color:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="color:#79C0FF"> string</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FFA657">  signal</span><span style="color:#FF7B72">?:</span><span style="color:#FFA657"> AbortSignal</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FFA657">  onProgress</span><span style="color:#FF7B72">?:</span><span style="color:#FFA657"> OnProgress</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E">  // Regla de oro, paso 2: cada par tiene su propio estado ('downloadable' si falta el paquete).</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">  const</span><span style="color:#79C0FF"> availability</span><span style="color:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="color:#FF7B72"> await</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> Translator.</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF">availability</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">({ sourceLanguage: source, targetLanguage: target });</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">  if</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> (availability </span><span style="color:#FF7B72">===</span><span style="color:#A5D6FF"> 'unavailable'</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">    throw</span><span style="color:#FF7B72"> new</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF"> Error</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">(</span><span style="color:#A5D6FF">`Par ${</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">source</span><span style="color:#A5D6FF">} → ${</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">target</span><span style="color:#A5D6FF">} no soportado.`</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">  }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">  const</span><span style="color:#79C0FF"> translator</span><span style="color:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="color:#FF7B72"> await</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF"> getTranslator</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">(source, target, onProgress);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E">  // El `signal` permite cancelar la traducción si el usuario sigue escribiendo.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">  return</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> translator.</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF">translateStreaming</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">(text, { signal });</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">}</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E"> * Demo 03 · Salida estructurada con JSON Schema.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E"> *</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E"> * El schema va en prompt() como `responseConstraint`, NO en create(). El</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E"> * navegador restringe la generación para que el modelo SOLO pueda producir JSON</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E"> * válido según el schema: nada de "Claro, aquí tienes el JSON: ```json…".</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E"> */</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">import</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> { ES, isAbortError } </span><span style="color:#FF7B72">from</span><span style="color:#A5D6FF"> './support'</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">;</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E">/** Tipo TypeScript que refleja el schema de abajo: lo que la UI recibe ya tipado. */</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">export</span><span style="color:#FF7B72"> interface</span><span style="color:#FFA657"> Review</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FFA657">  producto</span><span style="color:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="color:#79C0FF"> string</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FFA657">  sentimiento</span><span style="color:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="color:#A5D6FF"> 'positivo'</span><span style="color:#FF7B72"> |</span><span style="color:#A5D6FF"> 'neutral'</span><span style="color:#FF7B72"> |</span><span style="color:#A5D6FF"> 'negativo'</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FFA657">  puntaje</span><span style="color:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="color:#79C0FF"> number</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FFA657">  temas</span><span style="color:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="color:#79C0FF"> string</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">[];</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FFA657">  resumen</span><span style="color:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="color:#79C0FF"> string</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">}</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E">/** JSON Schema estándar: `enum`, `minimum`/`maximum`, `maxItems` y `required` se respetan al generar. */</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">export</span><span style="color:#FF7B72"> const</span><span style="color:#79C0FF"> reviewSchema</span><span style="color:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">  type: </span><span style="color:#A5D6FF">'object'</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">  properties: {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">    producto: { type: </span><span style="color:#A5D6FF">'string'</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> },</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">    sentimiento: { type: </span><span style="color:#A5D6FF">'string'</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">, enum: [</span><span style="color:#A5D6FF">'positivo'</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">, </span><span style="color:#A5D6FF">'neutral'</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">, </span><span style="color:#A5D6FF">'negativo'</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">] },</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">    puntaje: { type: </span><span style="color:#A5D6FF">'number'</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">, minimum: </span><span style="color:#79C0FF">1</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">, maximum: </span><span style="color:#79C0FF">5</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> },</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">    temas: { type: </span><span style="color:#A5D6FF">'array'</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">, items: { type: </span><span style="color:#A5D6FF">'string'</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> }, maxItems: </span><span style="color:#79C0FF">4</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> },</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">    resumen: { type: </span><span style="color:#A5D6FF">'string'</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> },</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">  },</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">  required: [</span><span style="color:#A5D6FF">'producto'</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">, </span><span style="color:#A5D6FF">'sentimiento'</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">, </span><span style="color:#A5D6FF">'puntaje'</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">, </span><span style="color:#A5D6FF">'temas'</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">, </span><span style="color:#A5D6FF">'resumen'</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">],</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">  additionalProperties: </span><span style="color:#79C0FF">false</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">} </span><span style="color:#FF7B72">as</span><span style="color:#FF7B72"> const</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">;</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E">/** Sesión base, creada una sola vez, con el prompt de sistema ya cargado. */</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">let</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> session</span><span style="color:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="color:#FFA657"> Promise</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">&#x3C;</span><span style="color:#FFA657">LanguageModel</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">> </span><span style="color:#FF7B72">|</span><span style="color:#79C0FF"> undefined</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">;</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">function</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF"> getSession</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">() {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">  session </span><span style="color:#FF7B72">??=</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> LanguageModel.</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF">create</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">({</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E">    // Declarar idioma de entrada/salida: availability() y la calidad dependen de ello.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">    expectedInputs: [{ type: </span><span style="color:#A5D6FF">'text'</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">, languages: </span><span style="color:#79C0FF">ES</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> }],</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">    expectedOutputs: [{ type: </span><span style="color:#A5D6FF">'text'</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">, languages: </span><span style="color:#79C0FF">ES</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> }],</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">    initialPrompts: [</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">      {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">        role: </span><span style="color:#A5D6FF">'system'</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">        content: </span><span style="color:#A5D6FF">'Analizas reseñas de productos y extraes datos. Responde siempre en español.'</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">      },</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">    ],</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">  });</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E">  // Si la creación falla (p. ej. falta activación), el siguiente clic reintenta.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">  session.</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF">catch</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">(() </span><span style="color:#FF7B72">=></span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> (session </span><span style="color:#FF7B72">=</span><span style="color:#79C0FF"> undefined</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">));</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">  return</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> session;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">}</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">export</span><span style="color:#FF7B72"> async</span><span style="color:#FF7B72"> function</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF"> analyzeReview</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">(</span><span style="color:#FFA657">review</span><span style="color:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="color:#79C0FF"> string</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">)</span><span style="color:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="color:#FFA657"> Promise</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">&#x3C;</span><span style="color:#FFA657">Review</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">  const</span><span style="color:#79C0FF"> sessionPromise</span><span style="color:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF"> getSession</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">();</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">  const</span><span style="color:#79C0FF"> s</span><span style="color:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="color:#FF7B72"> await</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> sessionPromise;</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E">  // Cada prompt() se suma al contexto de la sesión; clonamos para que los</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E">  // turnos no se acumulen (cada reseña se analiza "desde cero" con el mismo prompt de sistema).</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">  let</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> turn</span><span style="color:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="color:#FFA657"> LanguageModel</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">  try</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">    turn </span><span style="color:#FF7B72">=</span><span style="color:#FF7B72"> await</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> s.</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF">clone</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">();</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">  } </span><span style="color:#FF7B72">catch</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> (error) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E">    // La sesión base está rota: se expulsa para que el siguiente intento cree otra.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">    s.</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF">destroy</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">();</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">    if</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> (session </span><span style="color:#FF7B72">===</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> sessionPromise) session </span><span style="color:#FF7B72">=</span><span style="color:#79C0FF"> undefined</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">    throw</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> error;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">  }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">  try</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">    let</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> result</span><span style="color:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="color:#79C0FF"> string</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">    try</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> {</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">      result </span><span style="color:#FF7B72">=</span><span style="color:#FF7B72"> await</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> turn.</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF">prompt</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">(</span><span style="color:#A5D6FF">`Analiza esta reseña:</span><span style="color:#FF7B72">\n\n</span><span style="color:#A5D6FF">${</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">review</span><span style="color:#A5D6FF">}`</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">, {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">        responseConstraint: reviewSchema,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">      });</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">    } </span><span style="color:#FF7B72">catch</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> (error) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E">      // Un fallo en tiempo de ejecución puede dejar rota también la sesión base: se expulsa.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E">      // Los errores de JSON.parse de abajo son problemas de la salida, así que conservan la sesión.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">      if</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> (</span><span style="color:#FF7B72">!</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF">isAbortError</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">(error) </span><span style="color:#FF7B72">&#x26;&#x26;</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> session </span><span style="color:#FF7B72">===</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> sessionPromise) {</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">    return</span><span style="color:#79C0FF"> JSON</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">.</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF">parse</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">(result) </span><span style="color:#FF7B72">as</span><span style="color:#FFA657"> Review</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">;</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E">  // Si la creación falla, el siguiente intento vuelve a crear.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">  baseSession.</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF">catch</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">(() </span><span style="color:#FF7B72">=></span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> (baseSession </span><span style="color:#FF7B72">=</span><span style="color:#79C0FF"> undefined</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">));</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">}</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E">/** Expulsa la sesión base (p. ej. tras un fallo) para que la próxima llamada cree otra. */</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">function</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF"> dropBaseSession</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">()</span><span style="color:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="color:#79C0FF"> void</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> {</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E">/** ¿Se puede usar el modelo local ahora mismo? NUNCA lanza. */</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">async</span><span style="color:#FF7B72"> function</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF"> canUseLocal</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">(</span><span style="color:#FFA657">allowLocal</span><span style="color:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="color:#79C0FF"> true</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">)</span><span style="color:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="color:#FFA657"> Promise</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">&#x3C;</span><span style="color:#79C0FF">boolean</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">> {</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">async</span><span style="color:#FF7B72"> function</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF"> getLocalSession</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">(</span><span style="color:#FFA657">allowLocal</span><span style="color:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="color:#79C0FF"> true</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">)</span><span style="color:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="color:#FFA657"> Promise</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">&#x3C;</span><span style="color:#FFA657">LanguageModel</span><span style="color:#FF7B72"> |</span><span style="color:#79C0FF"> null</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">> {</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E">/** Estrategia manual: el patrón que puedes portar a cualquier backend (OpenAI, tu API, etc.). */</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">export</span><span style="color:#FF7B72"> async</span><span style="color:#FF7B72"> function</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF"> generateManual</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">(</span><span style="color:#FFA657">prompt</span><span style="color:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="color:#79C0FF"> string</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">, { </span><span style="color:#FFA657">allowLocal</span><span style="color:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="color:#79C0FF"> true</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> }</span><span style="color:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> { </span><span style="color:#FFA657">allowLocal</span><span style="color:#FF7B72">?:</span><span style="color:#79C0FF"> boolean</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> } </span><span style="color:#FF7B72">=</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> {})</span><span style="color:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="color:#FFA657"> Promise</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">&#x3C;</span><span style="color:#FFA657">Result</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">  const</span><span style="color:#79C0FF"> session</span><span style="color:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="color:#FF7B72"> await</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF"> getLocalSession</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">(allowLocal);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">  if</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> (session) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">    try</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">      return</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> { text: </span><span style="color:#FF7B72">await</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> session.</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF">prompt</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">(prompt), source: </span><span style="color:#A5D6FF">'on_device'</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> };</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">    } </span><span style="color:#FF7B72">catch</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> (error) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E">      // try/catch es la última red de seguridad: un fallo local en tiempo de ejecución cae a la nube.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">      if</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> (</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF">isAbortError</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">(error)) </span><span style="color:#FF7B72">throw</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> error; </span><span style="color:#8B949E">// una cancelación no debe gastar nube</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">      console.</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF">warn</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">(</span><span style="color:#A5D6FF">'[híbrido] Falló la inferencia local'</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">, error);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D2A8FF">      dropBaseSession</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">(); </span><span style="color:#8B949E">// un fallo en ejecución puede dejar rota también la base</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">    } </span><span style="color:#FF7B72">finally</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E">      // El clon es de un solo uso: se libera siempre. La base sigue viva.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">      session.</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF">destroy</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">();</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">    }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">  }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E">  // Esta línea en la consola es la prueba en escena de que entró la nube.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">  console.</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF">warn</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">(</span><span style="color:#A5D6FF">'[híbrido] Fallback a la nube'</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">  return</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> { text: </span><span style="color:#FF7B72">await</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF"> callCloud</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">(prompt), source: </span><span style="color:#A5D6FF">'in_cloud'</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> };</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">}</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E">/** Estrategia Firebase: un solo código, dos runtimes. El SDK decide y te dice quién respondió. */</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">export</span><span style="color:#FF7B72"> async</span><span style="color:#FF7B72"> function</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF"> generateFirebase</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">(</span><span style="color:#FFA657">prompt</span><span style="color:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="color:#79C0FF"> string</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">)</span><span style="color:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="color:#FFA657"> Promise</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">&#x3C;</span><span style="color:#FFA657">Result</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">  const</span><span style="color:#79C0FF"> mode</span><span style="color:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF"> forceCloud</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">() </span><span style="color:#FF7B72">?</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> InferenceMode.</span><span style="color:#79C0FF">ONLY_IN_CLOUD</span><span style="color:#FF7B72"> :</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> InferenceMode.</span><span style="color:#79C0FF">PREFER_ON_DEVICE</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">  const</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> { </span><span style="color:#79C0FF">response</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> } </span><span style="color:#FF7B72">=</span><span style="color:#FF7B72"> await</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF"> getHybridModel</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">(mode).</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF">generateContent</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">(prompt);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">  return</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> { text: response.</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF">text</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">(), source: (response.inferenceSource </span><span style="color:#FF7B72">as</span><span style="color:#FFA657"> Source</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">) </span><span style="color:#FF7B72">??</span><span style="color:#A5D6FF"> 'in_cloud'</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> };</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">}</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E"> */</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">import</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> { initializeApp, </span><span style="color:#FF7B72">type</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> FirebaseApp } </span><span style="color:#FF7B72">from</span><span style="color:#A5D6FF"> 'firebase/app'</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">;</span></span>

<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">declare</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> global {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">  var</span><span style="color:#79C0FF"> FIREBASE_APPCHECK_DEBUG_TOKEN</span><span style="color:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="color:#79C0FF"> boolean</span><span style="color:#FF7B72"> |</span><span style="color:#79C0FF"> string</span><span style="color:#FF7B72"> |</span><span style="color:#79C0FF"> undefined</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">;</span></span>

<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E">// Acceso literal a process.env para que Next.js inserte los valores en el bundle del cliente.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E">// (Son claves públicas de Firebase: la seguridad va en App Check y reglas, no en ocultarlas.)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">const</span><span style="color:#79C0FF"> config</span><span style="color:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">  apiKey: process.env.</span><span style="color:#79C0FF">NEXT_PUBLIC_FIREBASE_API_KEY</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">  authDomain: process.env.</span><span style="color:#79C0FF">NEXT_PUBLIC_FIREBASE_AUTH_DOMAIN</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">  projectId: process.env.</span><span style="color:#79C0FF">NEXT_PUBLIC_FIREBASE_PROJECT_ID</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">  appId: process.env.</span><span style="color:#79C0FF">NEXT_PUBLIC_FIREBASE_APP_ID</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">};</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">const</span><span style="color:#79C0FF"> recaptchaSiteKey</span><span style="color:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> process.env.</span><span style="color:#79C0FF">NEXT_PUBLIC_RECAPTCHA_SITE_KEY</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">;</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">export</span><span style="color:#FF7B72"> const</span><span style="color:#79C0FF"> isFirebaseConfigured</span><span style="color:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF"> Boolean</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">(config.apiKey </span><span style="color:#FF7B72">&#x26;&#x26;</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> config.projectId);</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">let</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> app</span><span style="color:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="color:#FFA657"> FirebaseApp</span><span style="color:#FF7B72"> |</span><span style="color:#79C0FF"> undefined</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">;</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E">/**</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E"> * App Check: el proxy de AI Logic rechaza (401) las requests sin token cuando el</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E"> * proyecto tiene enforcement activo. En localhost reCAPTCHA no valida, así que en</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E"> * desarrollo usamos un debug token registrado en la consola (App Check → Manage debug tokens).</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E"> * Sin NEXT_PUBLIC_FIREBASE_APPCHECK_DEBUG_TOKEN, el SDK genera uno y lo imprime en la consola del navegador.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E"> */</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">function</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF"> setupAppCheck</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">(</span><span style="color:#FFA657">firebaseApp</span><span style="color:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="color:#FFA657"> FirebaseApp</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">)</span><span style="color:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="color:#79C0FF"> void</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">  if</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> (</span><span style="color:#FF7B72">typeof</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> window </span><span style="color:#FF7B72">===</span><span style="color:#A5D6FF"> 'undefined'</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">) </span><span style="color:#FF7B72">return</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">  if</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> (</span><span style="color:#FF7B72">!</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">recaptchaSiteKey) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">    console.</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF">warn</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">(</span><span style="color:#A5D6FF">'[firebase] App Check desactivado: falta NEXT_PUBLIC_RECAPTCHA_SITE_KEY.'</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">    return</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">  }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">  if</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> (process.env.</span><span style="color:#79C0FF">NODE_ENV</span><span style="color:#FF7B72"> ===</span><span style="color:#A5D6FF"> 'development'</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E">    // Dentro de este branch para que el token no llegue al bundle de producción.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">    self.</span><span style="color:#79C0FF">FIREBASE_APPCHECK_DEBUG_TOKEN</span><span style="color:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> process.env.</span><span style="color:#79C0FF">NEXT_PUBLIC_FIREBASE_APPCHECK_DEBUG_TOKEN</span><span style="color:#FF7B72"> ||</span><span style="color:#79C0FF"> true</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">  }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D2A8FF">  initializeAppCheck</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">(firebaseApp, {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">    provider: </span><span style="color:#FF7B72">new</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF"> ReCaptchaEnterpriseProvider</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">(recaptchaSiteKey),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">    isTokenAutoRefreshEnabled: </span><span style="color:#79C0FF">true</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">  });</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">}</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E">/** Inicializa Firebase (y App Check) una sola vez y bajo demanda (solo si alguien llama a la nube). */</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">function</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF"> getApp</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">()</span><span style="color:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="color:#FFA657"> FirebaseApp</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">  if</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> (</span><span style="color:#FF7B72">!</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">isFirebaseConfigured) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">    throw</span><span style="color:#FF7B72"> new</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF"> Error</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">(</span><span style="color:#A5D6FF">'Firebase no configurado: copia example.env a .env.local.'</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">  }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">  if</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> (</span><span style="color:#FF7B72">!</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">app) {</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#D2A8FF">    setupAppCheck</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">(app);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">  }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">  return</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> app;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">}</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E">/**</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E"> * Modelo híbrido. `mode` decide quién responde:</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E"> *   PREFER_ON_DEVICE → Gemini Nano si está disponible, si no la nube (el que usa la demo)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E"> *   ONLY_IN_CLOUD    → siempre la nube (fallback manual y ?force=cloud)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E"> *   ONLY_ON_DEVICE   → nunca la nube</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E"> */</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">export</span><span style="color:#FF7B72"> function</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF"> getHybridModel</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">(</span><span style="color:#FFA657">mode</span><span style="color:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="color:#FFA657"> InferenceMode</span><span style="color:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> InferenceMode.</span><span style="color:#79C0FF">PREFER_ON_DEVICE</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">)</span><span style="color:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="color:#FFA657"> GenerativeModel</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">  const</span><span style="color:#79C0FF"> ai</span><span style="color:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF"> getAI</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">(</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF">getApp</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">(), { backend: </span><span style="color:#FF7B72">new</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF"> GoogleAIBackend</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">() });</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">  return</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF"> getGenerativeModel</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">(ai, {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">    mode,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E">    // Opcional: sin esto el SDK usa su modelo por defecto en la nube para el modo híbrido.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E">    // inCloudParams: { model: 'gemini-3.5-flash-lite' },</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E">    // Las mismas opciones que pasaríamos a LanguageModel.create() a mano.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">    onDeviceParams: {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">      createOptions: {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">        expectedInputs: [{ type: </span><span style="color:#A5D6FF">'text'</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">, languages: [</span><span style="color:#A5D6FF">'es'</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">] }],</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">        expectedOutputs: [{ type: </span><span style="color:#A5D6FF">'text'</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">, languages: [</span><span style="color:#A5D6FF">'es'</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">] }],</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">      },</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">    },</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">  });</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">}</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">export</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> { InferenceMode };</span></span>

<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E">/**</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E"> * Demo 01 · Ciclo de vida real y barra de progreso.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E"> *</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E"> * Muestra el estado de cada API y crea una sesión de la Prompt API mientras</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E"> * reporta en vivo la descarga del modelo.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E"> */</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">import</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> { useCallback, useEffect, useState } </span><span style="color:#FF7B72">from</span><span style="color:#A5D6FF"> "react"</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">;</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">import</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> { API_NAMES, ES, checkApi, progressMonitor, </span><span style="color:#FF7B72">type</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> ApiName, </span><span style="color:#FF7B72">type</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> ApiStatus } </span><span style="color:#FF7B72">from</span><span style="color:#A5D6FF"> "@/lib/ai/support"</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">import</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> { StatusPill } </span><span style="color:#FF7B72">from</span><span style="color:#A5D6FF"> "./status-pill"</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">;</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">export</span><span style="color:#FF7B72"> function</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF"> LifecycleDemo</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">() {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">  const</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> [</span><span style="color:#79C0FF">statuses</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">, </span><span style="color:#79C0FF">setStatuses</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">] </span><span style="color:#FF7B72">=</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF"> useState</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">&#x3C;</span><span style="color:#FFA657">Partial</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">&#x3C;</span><span style="color:#FFA657">Record</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">&#x3C;</span><span style="color:#FFA657">ApiName</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">, </span><span style="color:#FFA657">ApiStatus</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">>>>({});</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">  const</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> [</span><span style="color:#79C0FF">busy</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">, </span><span style="color:#79C0FF">setBusy</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">] </span><span style="color:#FF7B72">=</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF"> useState</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">(</span><span style="color:#79C0FF">false</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">);</span></span>

<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E">  // Consulta availability() de las 5 APIs en paralelo y llena la tabla.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">  const</span><span style="color:#79C0FF"> renderStatus</span><span style="color:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF"> useCallback</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">(</span><span style="color:#FF7B72">async</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> () </span><span style="color:#FF7B72">=></span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">    const</span><span style="color:#79C0FF"> results</span><span style="color:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="color:#FF7B72"> await</span><span style="color:#79C0FF"> Promise</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">.</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF">all</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">(</span><span style="color:#79C0FF">API_NAMES</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">.</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF">map</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">(checkApi));</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D2A8FF">    setStatuses</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">(Object.</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF">fromEntries</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">(</span><span style="color:#79C0FF">API_NAMES</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">.</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF">map</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">((</span><span style="color:#FFA657">name</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">, </span><span style="color:#FFA657">i</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">) </span><span style="color:#FF7B72">=></span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> [name, results[i]])));</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">  }, []);</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E">  // En un efecto, no en el render: los globals de IA no existen en el servidor.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D2A8FF">  useEffect</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">(() </span><span style="color:#FF7B72">=></span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">    void</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF"> renderStatus</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">();</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">  }, [renderStatus]);</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E">  // create() va dentro de un clic: si hay que descargar, exige activación del usuario.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">  async</span><span style="color:#FF7B72"> function</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF"> init</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">() {</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#D2A8FF">    setProgress</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">(</span><span style="color:#79C0FF">0</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">    output.</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF">show</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">(</span><span style="color:#A5D6FF">""</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">, </span><span style="color:#A5D6FF">"idle"</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">);</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">    try</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E">      // Regla de oro, paso 2: preguntar antes de crear.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">      const</span><span style="color:#79C0FF"> status</span><span style="color:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="color:#FF7B72"> await</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF"> checkApi</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">(</span><span style="color:#A5D6FF">"LanguageModel"</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D2A8FF">      setStatusText</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">(</span><span style="color:#A5D6FF">`availability() → ${</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">status</span><span style="color:#A5D6FF">}`</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">      if</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> (status </span><span style="color:#FF7B72">===</span><span style="color:#A5D6FF"> "no-soportada"</span><span style="color:#FF7B72"> ||</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> status </span><span style="color:#FF7B72">===</span><span style="color:#A5D6FF"> "unavailable"</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E">        // Este equipo no puede: es justo el puente hacia la Demo 06.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">        throw</span><span style="color:#FF7B72"> new</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF"> Error</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">(</span><span style="color:#A5D6FF">"Prompt API no disponible en este equipo. Ver Demo 06 (fallback)."</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">      }</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E">      // Paso 3: create() con monitor → progreso real, no un spinner infinito.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">      const</span><span style="color:#79C0FF"> session</span><span style="color:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="color:#FF7B72"> await</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> LanguageModel.</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF">create</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">({</span></span>
