<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">        expectedOutputs: [{ type: </span><span style="color:#A5D6FF">"text"</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">, languages: </span><span style="color:#79C0FF">ES</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> }],</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">        monitor: </span><span style="color:#D2A8FF">progressMonitor</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">((</span><span style="color:#FFA657">fraction</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">) </span><span style="color:#FF7B72">=></span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">          const</span><span style="color:#79C0FF"> pct</span><span style="color:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> Math.</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF">round</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">(fraction </span><span style="color:#FF7B72">*</span><span style="color:#79C0FF"> 100</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D2A8FF">          setProgress</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">(pct);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D2A8FF">          setStatusText</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">(</span><span style="color:#A5D6FF">`Descargando modelo: ${</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">pct</span><span style="color:#A5D6FF">}%`</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">        }),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">      });</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E">      // create() resolvió: el modelo está listo (con o sin eventos de progreso).</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D2A8FF">      setProgress</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">(</span><span style="color:#79C0FF">100</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D2A8FF">      setStatusText</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">(</span><span style="color:#A5D6FF">"Modelo listo ✅"</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">      try</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E">        // Prueba de vida: un prompt corto que demuestra que la sesión responde.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">        output.</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF">show</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">(</span><span style="color:#FF7B72">await</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> session.</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF">prompt</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">(</span><span style="color:#A5D6FF">"Saluda a una audiencia de desarrolladores en una sola frase."</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">));</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">      } </span><span style="color:#FF7B72">finally</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E">        // Las sesiones ocupan memoria del modelo: se destruyen al terminar.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">        session.</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF">destroy</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">();</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">      }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">    } </span><span style="color:#FF7B72">catch</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> (error) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">      output.</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF">fail</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">(error);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">    } </span><span style="color:#FF7B72">finally</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D2A8FF">      setBusy</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">(</span><span style="color:#79C0FF">false</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E">      // Refresca la tabla: tras una descarga, 'downloadable' pasa a 'available'.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">      void</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF"> renderStatus</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">();</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">    }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">  }</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">  return</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> (</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">    &#x3C;</span><span style="color:#7EE787">div</span><span style="color:#79C0FF"> className</span><span style="color:#FF7B72">=</span><span style="color:#A5D6FF">"grid gap-6 md:grid-cols-2"</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">></span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">      &#x3C;</span><span style="color:#7EE787">div</span><span style="color:#79C0FF"> className</span><span style="color:#FF7B72">=</span><span style="color:#A5D6FF">"flex flex-col gap-3"</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">></span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">        &#x3C;</span><span style="color:#7EE787">table</span><span style="color:#79C0FF"> className</span><span style="color:#FF7B72">=</span><span style="color:#A5D6FF">"w-full border-collapse"</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">></span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">          &#x3C;</span><span style="color:#7EE787">thead</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">></span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">            &#x3C;</span><span style="color:#7EE787">tr</span><span style="color:#79C0FF"> className</span><span style="color:#FF7B72">=</span><span style="color:#A5D6FF">"text-left text-xs font-medium text-ink-300"</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">></span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">              &#x3C;</span><span style="color:#7EE787">th</span><span style="color:#79C0FF"> className</span><span style="color:#FF7B72">=</span><span style="color:#A5D6FF">"border-b border-ink-600 px-2 py-2.5"</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">>API&#x3C;/</span><span style="color:#7EE787">th</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">></span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E">/**</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#A5D6FF">  "The best part of on-device AI is that your users' data never leaves their laptop. No API keys, no tokens, no invoices."</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">;</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">export</span><span style="color:#FF7B72"> function</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF"> TranslateDemo</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">() {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">  const</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> [</span><span style="color:#79C0FF">text</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">, </span><span style="color:#79C0FF">setText</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">] </span><span style="color:#FF7B72">=</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF"> useState</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">(</span><span style="color:#79C0FF">INITIAL</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">  const</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> [</span><span style="color:#79C0FF">target</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">, </span><span style="color:#79C0FF">setTarget</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">] </span><span style="color:#FF7B72">=</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF"> useState</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">(</span><span style="color:#A5D6FF">"es"</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">  const</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> [</span><span style="color:#79C0FF">detected</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">, </span><span style="color:#79C0FF">setDetected</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">] </span><span style="color:#FF7B72">=</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF"> useState</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">(</span><span style="color:#A5D6FF">"Idioma detectado: —"</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">  const</span><span style="color:#79C0FF"> output</span><span style="color:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF"> useOutput</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">();</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E">  // Una ejecución en curso a la vez: cada nueva cancela la anterior.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">  const</span><span style="color:#79C0FF"> controller</span><span style="color:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF"> useRef</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">&#x3C;</span><span style="color:#FFA657">AbortController</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">>();</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">  const</span><span style="color:#79C0FF"> debounceTimer</span><span style="color:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF"> useRef</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">&#x3C;</span><span style="color:#FFA657">ReturnType</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">&#x3C;</span><span style="color:#FF7B72">typeof</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> setTimeout>>();</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E">  // Al salir de la página: cancela el debounce y la traducción pendiente.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D2A8FF">  useEffect</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">(</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">    () </span><span style="color:#FF7B72">=></span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> () </span><span style="color:#FF7B72">=></span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D2A8FF">      clearTimeout</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">(debounceTimer.current);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">      controller.current?.</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF">abort</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">();</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">    },</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">    [],</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">  );</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">  async</span><span style="color:#FF7B72"> function</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF"> run</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">(</span><span style="color:#FFA657">value</span><span style="color:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="color:#79C0FF"> string</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">, </span><span style="color:#FFA657">targetLanguage</span><span style="color:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="color:#79C0FF"> string</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E">    // Cancelar primero, para que una ejecución vieja nunca pinte sobre un input vacío.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">    controller.current?.</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF">abort</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">();</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">    const</span><span style="color:#79C0FF"> input</span><span style="color:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> value.</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF">trim</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">();</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">    if</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> (</span><span style="color:#FF7B72">!</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">input) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">      controller.current </span><span style="color:#FF7B72">=</span><span style="color:#79C0FF"> undefined</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D2A8FF">      setDetected</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">(</span><span style="color:#A5D6FF">"Idioma detectado: —"</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">      output.</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF">show</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">(</span><span style="color:#A5D6FF">""</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">, </span><span style="color:#A5D6FF">"idle"</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">      return</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">    }</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">    controller.current </span><span style="color:#FF7B72">=</span><span style="color:#FF7B72"> new</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF"> AbortController</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">();</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">    const</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> { </span><span style="color:#79C0FF">signal</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> } </span><span style="color:#FF7B72">=</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> controller.current;</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E">    // Arranque en frío: create() puede descargar un modelo, así que mostramos progreso en vez de un panel en blanco.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">    const</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF"> onProgress</span><span style="color:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> (</span><span style="color:#FFA657">fraction</span><span style="color:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="color:#79C0FF"> number</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">) </span><span style="color:#FF7B72">=></span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">      if</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> (</span><span style="color:#FF7B72">!</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">signal.aborted) output.</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF">show</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">(</span><span style="color:#A5D6FF">`Preparando modelo… ${</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">Math</span><span style="color:#A5D6FF">.</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF">round</span><span style="color:#A5D6FF">(</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">fraction</span><span style="color:#FF7B72"> *</span><span style="color:#79C0FF"> 100</span><span style="color:#A5D6FF">)</span><span style="color:#A5D6FF">}%`</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">, </span><span style="color:#A5D6FF">"streaming"</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">    };</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">    if</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> (</span><span style="color:#FF7B72">!</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">output.text) output.</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF">show</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">(</span><span style="color:#A5D6FF">"Preparando modelo…"</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">, </span><span style="color:#A5D6FF">"streaming"</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">);</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E">    // Guarda el paso actual para que un fallo genérico diga dónde ocurrió (mirar la consola en escena).</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">    let</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> step </span><span style="color:#FF7B72">=</span><span style="color:#A5D6FF"> "support check"</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">    let</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> pair</span><span style="color:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> [</span><span style="color:#79C0FF">string</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">, </span><span style="color:#79C0FF">string</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">] </span><span style="color:#FF7B72">|</span><span style="color:#79C0FF"> undefined</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">    try</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E">      // Regla de oro, paso 1: feature detection.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">      if</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> (</span><span style="color:#FF7B72">!</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF">hasApi</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">(</span><span style="color:#A5D6FF">"LanguageDetector"</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">) </span><span style="color:#FF7B72">||</span><span style="color:#FF7B72"> !</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF">hasApi</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">(</span><span style="color:#A5D6FF">"Translator"</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">)) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">        throw</span><span style="color:#FF7B72"> new</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF"> Error</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">(</span><span style="color:#A5D6FF">"Translator / LanguageDetector no soportados (solo Chrome desktop)."</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">      }</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E">      // 1) ¿En qué idioma escribió el usuario? Sin selector de origen.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">      step </span><span style="color:#FF7B72">=</span><span style="color:#A5D6FF"> "detect"</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">      const</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> { </span><span style="color:#79C0FF">language</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">, </span><span style="color:#79C0FF">confidence</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> } </span><span style="color:#FF7B72">=</span><span style="color:#FF7B72"> await</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF"> detectLanguage</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">(input, signal, onProgress);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D2A8FF">      setDetected</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">(</span><span style="color:#A5D6FF">`Idioma detectado: ${</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">language</span><span style="color:#A5D6FF">} (${</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">confidence</span><span style="color:#A5D6FF">}%)`</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">);</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E">      // Mismo idioma de origen y destino: no hay nada que traducir.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">      if</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> (language </span><span style="color:#FF7B72">===</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> targetLanguage) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">        output.</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF">show</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">(input);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">        return</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">      }</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E">      // 2) Traducir con streaming: el texto aparece mientras se genera.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">      pair </span><span style="color:#FF7B72">=</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> [language, targetLanguage];</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">      step </span><span style="color:#FF7B72">=</span><span style="color:#A5D6FF"> `translator ${</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">language</span><span style="color:#A5D6FF">}→${</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">targetLanguage</span><span style="color:#A5D6FF">}`</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">      const</span><span style="color:#79C0FF"> stream</span><span style="color:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="color:#FF7B72"> await</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF"> translateStreaming</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">(input, language, targetLanguage, signal, onProgress);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">      step </span><span style="color:#FF7B72">=</span><span style="color:#A5D6FF"> `stream ${</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">language</span><span style="color:#A5D6FF">}→${</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">targetLanguage</span><span style="color:#A5D6FF">}`</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">      await</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF"> streamText</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">(stream, output.paint, signal);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">      if</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> (</span><span style="color:#FF7B72">!</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">signal.aborted) output.</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF">setState</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">(</span><span style="color:#A5D6FF">"done"</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">    } </span><span style="color:#FF7B72">catch</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> (error) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E">      // Una ejecución superada nunca debe expulsar la instancia de la ejecución más nueva.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">      if</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> (signal.aborted </span><span style="color:#FF7B72">||</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF"> isAbortError</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">(error)) </span><span style="color:#FF7B72">return</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">      console.</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF">error</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">(</span><span style="color:#A5D6FF">`[translate] failed at step: ${</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">step</span><span style="color:#A5D6FF">}`</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E">      // Expulsa lo que falló para que el siguiente intento se cure solo, sin recargar la página.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">      if</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> (</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF">isRuntimeFailure</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">(error)) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">        if</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> (pair) </span><span style="color:#D2A8FF">resetTranslator</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">(</span><span style="color:#FF7B72">...</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">pair);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">        else</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF"> resetDetector</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">();</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">      }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">      output.</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF">fail</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">(error);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">    }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">  }</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E">  // Traducción "en tiempo real": espera una pausa de 400 ms al escribir.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E">  // La tecla cuenta como activación del usuario, así que create() puede descargar si hace falta.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">  function</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF"> onInput</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">(</span><span style="color:#FFA657">value</span><span style="color:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="color:#79C0FF"> string</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D2A8FF">    setText</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">(value);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D2A8FF">    clearTimeout</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">(debounceTimer.current);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">    debounceTimer.current </span><span style="color:#FF7B72">=</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF"> setTimeout</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">(() </span><span style="color:#FF7B72">=></span><span style="color:#FF7B72"> void</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF"> run</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">(value, target), </span><span style="color:#79C0FF">400</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">  }</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E">  // Cambiar el idioma destino traduce de inmediato (sin debounce).</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E">  // Cancela antes el debounce pendiente: su closure guarda el destino anterior y,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E">  // al dispararse, abortaría esta ejecución y traduciría al idioma viejo.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E">  // `text` ya tiene lo último escrito, así que no se pierde nada.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">  function</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF"> onTarget</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">(</span><span style="color:#FFA657">value</span><span style="color:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="color:#79C0FF"> string</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D2A8FF">    clearTimeout</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">(debounceTimer.current);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D2A8FF">    setTarget</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">(value);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">    void</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF"> run</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">(text, value);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">  }</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">  return</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> (</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">    &#x3C;</span><span style="color:#7EE787">div</span><span style="color:#79C0FF"> className</span><span style="color:#FF7B72">=</span><span style="color:#A5D6FF">"grid gap-6 md:grid-cols-2"</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">></span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">      &#x3C;</span><span style="color:#7EE787">div</span><span style="color:#79C0FF"> className</span><span style="color:#FF7B72">=</span><span style="color:#A5D6FF">"flex flex-col gap-3"</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">></span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">        &#x3C;</span><span style="color:#7EE787">textarea</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#79C0FF">          className</span><span style="color:#FF7B72">=</span><span style="color:#A5D6FF">"field resize-y leading-relaxed"</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#79C0FF">          rows</span><span style="color:#FF7B72">={</span><span style="color:#79C0FF">6</span><span style="color:#FF7B72">}</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#79C0FF">          value</span><span style="color:#FF7B72">={</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">text</span><span style="color:#FF7B72">}</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#79C0FF">          onChange</span><span style="color:#FF7B72">={</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">(</span><span style="color:#FFA657">e</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">) </span><span style="color:#FF7B72">=></span><span style="color:#D2A8FF"> onInput</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">(e.target.value)</span><span style="color:#FF7B72">}</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">        /></span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">        &#x3C;</span><span style="color:#7EE787">label</span><span style="color:#79C0FF"> className</span><span style="color:#FF7B72">=</span><span style="color:#A5D6FF">"flex items-center gap-3 text-ink-300"</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">></span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">          Traducir a</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">          &#x3C;</span><span style="color:#7EE787">select</span><span style="color:#79C0FF"> className</span><span style="color:#FF7B72">=</span><span style="color:#A5D6FF">"field w-auto"</span><span style="color:#79C0FF"> value</span><span style="color:#FF7B72">={</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">target</span><span style="color:#FF7B72">}</span><span style="color:#79C0FF"> onChange</span><span style="color:#FF7B72">={</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">(</span><span style="color:#FFA657">e</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">) </span><span style="color:#FF7B72">=></span><span style="color:#D2A8FF"> onTarget</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">(e.target.value)</span><span style="color:#FF7B72">}</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">></span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">            {</span><span style="color:#79C0FF">TARGETS</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">.</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF">map</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">((</span><span style="color:#FFA657">t</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">) </span><span style="color:#FF7B72">=></span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> (</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">              &#x3C;</span><span style="color:#7EE787">option</span><span style="color:#79C0FF"> key</span><span style="color:#FF7B72">={</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">t.value</span><span style="color:#FF7B72">}</span><span style="color:#79C0FF"> value</span><span style="color:#FF7B72">={</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">t.value</span><span style="color:#FF7B72">}</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">></span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">                {</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">t.label</span><span style="color:#FF7B72">}</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">              &#x3C;/</span><span style="color:#7EE787">option</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">></span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">            ))</span><span style="color:#FF7B72">}</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">          &#x3C;/</span><span style="color:#7EE787">select</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">></span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">        &#x3C;/</span><span style="color:#7EE787">label</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">></span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">      &#x3C;/</span><span style="color:#7EE787">div</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">></span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">      &#x3C;</span><span style="color:#7EE787">div</span><span style="color:#79C0FF"> className</span><span style="color:#FF7B72">=</span><span style="color:#A5D6FF">"flex flex-col gap-3"</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">></span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">        &#x3C;</span><span style="color:#7EE787">p</span><span style="color:#79C0FF"> className</span><span style="color:#FF7B72">=</span><span style="color:#A5D6FF">"meta"</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">></span><span style="color:#FF7B72">{</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">detected</span><span style="color:#FF7B72">}</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">&#x3C;/</span><span style="color:#7EE787">p</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">></span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">        &#x3C;</span><span style="color:#7EE787">Output</span><span style="color:#79C0FF"> text</span><span style="color:#FF7B72">={</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">output.text</span><span style="color:#FF7B72">}</span><span style="color:#79C0FF"> state</span><span style="color:#FF7B72">={</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">output.state</span><span style="color:#FF7B72">}</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> /></span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">      &#x3C;/</span><span style="color:#7EE787">div</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">></span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">    &#x3C;/</span><span style="color:#7EE787">div</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">></span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">  );</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">}</span></span>

<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E">// El texto de ejemplo resume la propia tesis de la charla: costo, latencia, privacidad y puente híbrido.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">const</span><span style="color:#79C0FF"> INITIAL</span><span style="color:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="color:#A5D6FF"> `Durante años, agregar inteligencia artificial a una aplicación web significaba lo mismo: enviar los datos del usuario a un servidor, pagar por cada token procesado y esperar la respuesta de la red. Ese modelo funciona, pero tiene tres costos que crecen con la escala: la factura de la nube, la latencia percibida y el riesgo regulatorio de mover datos personales fuera del dispositivo.</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#A5D6FF">Chrome cambió esa ecuación al integrar Gemini Nano directamente en el navegador. Ahora existen APIs estándar para traducir, resumir, reescribir y generar texto estructurado que se ejecutan en el hardware del usuario. El primer uso descarga el modelo una sola vez; a partir de ahí, cada inferencia es local, gratuita y funciona sin conexión.</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#A5D6FF">El reto ya no es técnico sino de arquitectura. No todos los equipos tienen el hardware necesario, algunas empresas bloquean la descarga por política, y las APIs avanzan a ritmos distintos entre versiones de Chrome. Por eso los equipos que adoptan IA local con éxito diseñan desde el inicio un puente híbrido: inferencia en el cliente cuando es posible y respaldo en la nube cuando no.`</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">;</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">const</span><span style="color:#79C0FF"> TYPES</span><span style="color:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="color:#FFA657"> SummarizerType</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">[] </span><span style="color:#FF7B72">=</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> [</span><span style="color:#A5D6FF">"key-points"</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">, </span><span style="color:#A5D6FF">"tldr"</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">, </span><span style="color:#A5D6FF">"teaser"</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">, </span><span style="color:#A5D6FF">"headline"</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">];</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">const</span><span style="color:#79C0FF"> LENGTHS</span><span style="color:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="color:#FFA657"> SummarizerLength</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">[] </span><span style="color:#FF7B72">=</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> [</span><span style="color:#A5D6FF">"short"</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">, </span><span style="color:#A5D6FF">"medium"</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">, </span><span style="color:#A5D6FF">"long"</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">];</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">        throw</span><span style="color:#FF7B72"> new</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF"> Error</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">(</span><span style="color:#A5D6FF">"El texto supera la cuota. <span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">      }</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">  const</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> [</span><span style="color:#79C0FF">text</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">, </span><span style="color:#79C0FF">setText</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">] </span><span style="color:#FF7B72">=</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF"> useState</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">(initial);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">  const</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> [</span><span style="color:#79C0FF">state</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">, </span><span style="color:#79C0FF">setState</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">] </span><span style="color:#FF7B72">=</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF"> useState</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">&#x3C;</span><span style="color:#FFA657">OutputState</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">>(</span><span style="color:#A5D6FF">"idle"</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">);</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">  const</span><span style="color:#79C0FF"> show</span><span style="color:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF"> useCallback</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">((</span><span style="color:#FFA657">value</span><span style="color:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="color:#79C0FF"> string</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">, </span><span style="color:#FFA657">next</span><span style="color:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="color:#FFA657"> OutputState</span><span style="color:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="color:#A5D6FF"> "done"</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">) </span><span style="color:#FF7B72">=></span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D2A8FF">    setText</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">(value);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D2A8FF">    setState</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">(next);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">  }, []);</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">  const</span><span style="color:#79C0FF"> fail</span><span style="color:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF"> useCallback</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">((</span><span style="color:#FFA657">error</span><span style="color:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="color:#79C0FF"> unknown</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">) </span><span style="color:#FF7B72">=></span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E">    // Log the full exception so DevTools shows its name, stack and cause.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">    console.</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF">error</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">(</span><span style="color:#A5D6FF">"[demo]"</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">, error);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">    const</span><span style="color:#79C0FF"> name</span><span style="color:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> error </span><span style="color:#FF7B72">instanceof</span><span style="color:#FFA657"> Error</span><span style="color:#FF7B72"> &#x26;&#x26;</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> error.name </span><span style="color:#FF7B72">!==</span><span style="color:#A5D6FF"> "Error"</span><span style="color:#FF7B72"> ?</span><span style="color:#A5D6FF"> `${</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">error</span><span style="color:#A5D6FF">.</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">name</span><span style="color:#A5D6FF">}: `</span><span style="color:#FF7B72"> :</span><span style="color:#A5D6FF"> ""</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D2A8FF">    setText</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">(</span><span style="color:#A5D6FF">`⚠️ ${</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">name</span><span style="color:#A5D6FF">}${</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF">errorMessage</span><span style="color:#A5D6FF">(</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">error</span><span style="color:#A5D6FF">)</span><span style="color:#A5D6FF">}`</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D2A8FF">    setState</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">(</span><span style="color:#A5D6FF">"error"</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">  }, []);</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8B949E">  /** Paint callback for streamText(): marks the panel as streaming. */</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">  const</span><span style="color:#79C0FF"> paint</span><span style="color:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF"> useCallback</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">((</span><span style="color:#FFA657">value</span><span style="color:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="color:#79C0FF"> string</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">) </span><span style="color:#FF7B72">=></span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D2A8FF">    setText</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">(value);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#D2A8FF">    setState</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">(</span><span style="color:#A5D6FF">"streaming"</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">  }, []);</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">  return</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> { text, state, show, fail, paint, setState };</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">}</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">export</span><span style="color:#FF7B72"> function</span><span style="color:#D2A8FF"> Output</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">({</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FFA657">  text</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FFA657">  state</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FFA657">  mono</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">}</span><span style="color:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FFA657">  text</span><span style="color:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="color:#79C0FF"> string</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FFA657">  state</span><span style="color:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="color:#FFA657"> OutputState</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FFA657">  mono</span><span style="color:#FF7B72">?:</span><span style="color:#79C0FF"> boolean</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">}) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">  return</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3"> (</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">    &#x3C;</span><span style="color:#7EE787">pre</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#79C0FF">      className</span><span style="color:#FF7B72">={</span><span style="color:#A5D6FF">`out ${</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">mono</span><span style="color:#FF7B72"> ?</span><span style="color:#A5D6FF"> "font-mono text-sm"</span><span style="color:#FF7B72"> :</span><span style="color:#A5D6FF"> ""}`</span><span style="color:#FF7B72">}</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#79C0FF">      data-state</span><span style="color:#FF7B72">={</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">state</span><span style="color:#FF7B72">}</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#79C0FF">      aria-live</span><span style="color:#FF7B72">=</span><span style="color:#A5D6FF">"polite"</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">    ></span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF7B72">      {</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">text</span><span style="color:#FF7B72">}</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">    &#x3C;/</span><span style="color:#7EE787">pre</span><span style="color:#E6EDF3">></span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">  );</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E6EDF3">}</span></span>
